# ALO Labs complete source file inventory

**Captured:** 2026-10-09 (Asia/Dhaka). **Scope:** `HEAD` at `07668db` plus current nonignored working-tree files before this audit update. This is a repository-source inventory, not a list of `node_modules`, `.next`, generated caches, ignored local databases, uploaded private files, or `.env` secrets. No files have been removed by this inventory.

**Important:** Baseline inventory did not precede the earlier AgentOS implementation pass; this is a retrospective snapshot, not evidence that its Phase 0 gate passed. Static references capture a subset of TS/JS imports only (not dynamic route calls or deployment references). `NOT VERIFIED` means no per-file runtime/security validation is claimed. `No static TS import found` does **not** mean a file is orphaned. Dependencies list up to three import specifiers, not a full transitive dependency graph.

Baseline HEAD files: **1056**; current nonignored files at capture: **1131**; baseline missing: **0**. New generated inventory is included below.
